Model before rewriting:

find x: int(2..4)
find y: int(2..4)
find z: int(2..4)

such that

product([product([x,y;int(1..)]),z;int(1..)]) < 15

--

z
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
z
new variables:
  find z#sign_magnitude_1: bool
  find z#sign_magnitude_2: bool
  find z#sign_magnitude_3: bool
  find z#sign_magnitude_4: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(z#sign_magnitude_3),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_2),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  !(z#sign_magnitude_4)
  (!(z#sign_magnitude_4)) -> (or([!(z#sign_magnitude_3),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_2),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  (z#sign_magnitude_4) -> (or([z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2,z#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)]))
  or([and([SATInt(SignMagnitude, [z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2,z#sign_magnitude_3,z#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [2, 4]) >= 2,SATInt(SignMagnitude, [z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2,z#sign_magnitude_3,z#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [2, 4]) <= 4;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

SATInt(SignMagnitude, [z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2,z#sign_magnitude_3,z#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [2, 4]) >= 2
   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
__0
new variables:
  findAux __0: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__0 <-> integer-weighted(1*z#sign_magnitude_1, -2*and([z#sign_magnitude_4,z#sign_magnitude_1;int(1..)]), 2*z#sign_magnitude_2, -4*and([z#sign_magnitude_4,z#sign_magnitude_2;int(1..)]), 4*z#sign_magnitude_3, -8*and([z#sign_magnitude_4,z#sign_magnitude_3;int(1..)])) GreaterEqual 2) using None with []

--

SATInt(SignMagnitude, [z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2,z#sign_magnitude_3,z#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)] [2, 4]) <= 4
   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
